Run health, then work through the unmet requirements that need judgement, fixing each with the user's consent, asking for the ticket project and knowledge base and finding a connector for them, listing what cannot be fixed with its remedy, and running health again. ([DevPossible/aa-sdlc](https://github.com/DevPossible/aa-sdlc/tree/HEAD/src/aa-sdlc/skills/framework/init))
- **init**: Set up autofix in this project. Detects repos, base branches and verification commands, writes autofix.config.json, and checks that the budget status line is wired up. Run once before /dev-autofix:run or /dev-autofix:plan. ([DigitalFuelCapital/dfc-dev-plugins](https://github.com/DigitalFuelCapital/dfc-dev-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/dev-autofix/skills/init))
- **init**: Set up specsteward in the current repository - detects the stack and the project's own lint, type and test commands, writes sdd.toml, spec/plan/tasks templates, the .gitignore entry and permission rules (approve, trust, start, resume, signoff, commit, push, PR create and PR merge stay behind "ask"), then checks the setup with `sdd doctor`. Use for "/specsteward:init". ([Dinusha-Ekanayake/specsteward](https://github.com/Dinusha-Ekanayake/specsteward/tree/HEAD/skills/init))
